d4:T4ce,Answer: $[\text{CH}_{3}\text{CO}_{2}^{\text{−}}]$

(a) The added HCl will increase the concentration of H 3 O + slightly, which will react with $\text{CH}_{3}\text{CO}_{2}^{\text{−}}$ and produce CH 3 CO 2 H in the process. Thus, $[\text{CH}_{3}\text{CO}_{2}^{\text{−}}]$ decreases and [CH 3 CO 2 H] increases.
(b) The added KCH 3 CO 2 will increase the concentration of $[\text{CH}_{3}\text{CO}_{2}^{\text{−}}]$ which will react with H 3 O + and produce CH 3 CO 2 H in the process. Thus, [H 3 O + ] decreases slightly and [CH 3 CO 2 H] increases.
(c) The added NaCl will have no effect on the concentration of the ions.
(d) The added KOH will produce OH − ions, which will react with the H 3 O + , thus reducing [H 3 O + ]. Some additional CH 3 CO 2 H will dissociate, producing $[\text{CH}_{3}\text{CO}_{2}^{\text{−}}]$ ions in the process. Thus, [CH 3 CO 2 H] decreases slightly and $[\text{CH}_{3}\text{CO}_{2}^{\text{−}}]$ increases.
(e) The added CH 3 CO 2 H will increase its concentration, causing more of it to dissociate and producing more $[\text{CH}_{3}\text{CO}_{2}^{\text{−}}]$ and H 3 O + in the process. Thus, [H 3 O + ] increases slightly and $[\text{CH}_{3}\text{CO}_{2}^{\text{−}}]$ increases.d0:["$","li","openstax-chemistry-atoms-first-2e-ch14-fs-idm108992432",{"className":"rounded-md border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 p-3 text-sm","children":[["$","div",null,{"className":"flex items-baseline justify-between gap-2 flex-wrap mb-1.5","children":[["$","span",null,{"className":"text-[11px] font-mono text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400","children":["#",23," · ","openstax-chemistry-atoms-first-2e-ch14-fs-idm108992432"]}],["$","div",null,{"className":"flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[10px] text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 flex-wrap","children":[["$","span",null,{"className":"px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-800","children":"hard"}],false]}]]}],["$","p",null,{"className":"text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100 leading-snug whitespace-pre-wrap mb-2","children":"84 .
